<script type="text/javascript">
(function (retailrocket) {
  'use strict';

  var waitFor = function (exitCondition, callback, force) {
    var checkCount = 100;
    var timeout = 1000;

    (function check() {
      var result = exitCondition();
      if (result) {
        callback(result);
        return;
      }

      if (checkCount === 0) {
        if (force) {
          callback();
        }
        return;
      }

      checkCount -= 1;
      setTimeout(check, timeout);
    }());
  };

  var СarouselModel = function (sliderArguments) {
    var defaultArguments = {
      wrapSelector: '.retailrocket-items',
      carouselSettings: {},
      init: false,
    };
    var carousel = {};
    var carouselSource = 'https://rrstatic.retailrocket.net/widget/plugins/swiper/mod_no_conf/rrswiper-noconf.min.js';

    function render(callback) {
      function carouselInit() {
        var extend = function(out) {
          out = out || {};

          for (var i = 1; i < arguments.length; i++) {
            if (!arguments[i])
              continue;

            for (var key in arguments[i]) {
              if (arguments[i].hasOwnProperty(key))
                out[key] = arguments[i][key];
            }
          }

          return out;
        };

        var settings = extend({}, defaultArguments, sliderArguments);
        var Nwidget = settings.widget;

        carousel = new RRSwiper(Nwidget.querySelector(settings.wrapSelector), settings.carouselSettings)
          .on('init', function() {
          Nwidget.classList.add('rr-active');
          Nwidget.querySelectorAll('.retailrocket-item').forEach(function (item) {
            item.querySelector('.item-image img').addEventListener('error', function () {
              var slideItem = item;
              var $slideSiblings = item.parentNode.querySelectorAll('.swiper-slide:not(".swiper-slide-duplicate")');
              $slideSiblings.forEach(function (sItem,index) {
                sItem.setAttribute('rrindex',(index + 1))
              });

              var index = slideItem.getAttribute('rrindex');

              if (index !== -1) {
                carousel.removeSlide(index);
              }
            });
          });
        });

        carousel.init();

        if (typeof callback === 'function') {
          callback(carousel);
        }
      }

      waitFor(
        function () {
          return window.RRSwiper;
        },
        function () {
          carouselInit();
        }
      );
    }

    if (!('widget' in sliderArguments)) {
      throw new Error('The widget is required');
    }

    this.render = render;
  };

  if (!('duplicates' in retailrocket.modules)) {
    retailrocket.setModule('duplicates', [], function () {
      var savedIds = [];
      var paramName = 'ItemId';

      function has(id) {
        return (savedIds.indexOf(id) !== -1);
      }

      function add(id) {
        savedIds = savedIds.concat(id);
      }

      function remove(id) {
        var ids = [].concat(id);

        savedIds = savedIds.filter(function (savedId) {
          return ids.indexOf(savedId) === -1;
        });
      }

      function filter(recom) {
        if (!recom[paramName]) {
          return true;
        }

        if (!has(recom[paramName])) {
          add(recom[paramName]);
          return true;
        }

        return false;
      }

      function filteredCount(recoms) {
        return recoms.reduce(function (acc, recom) {
          return (acc + (has(recom[paramName]) ? 1 : 0));
        }, 0);
      }

      function getItems() {
        return savedIds.slice();
      }

      return {
        has: has,
        add: add,
        remove: remove,
        filter: filter,
        filteredCount: filteredCount,
        getItems: getItems,
      };
    });
  }

  retailrocket['meleon5c936b1697a5250c04c71061'] = (function () {
    function preRenderFn(widget, recoms, renderFn) {
      var filteredRecoms = recoms.filter(retailrocket.modules.duplicates.filter);

      if (filteredRecoms.length > 4) {
        renderFn(filteredRecoms);
      }  else {
        retailrocket.widget.render(widget.getAttribute('data-next'));
      }
    }

    function postRenderFn(widget) {
      var carousel = new СarouselModel({
        widget: widget,
        carouselSettings: {
          containerModifierClass: 'retailrocket-items',
          wrapperClass: 'rr-swiper-wrapper',
          slideClass: 'retailrocket-item',
          init: false,
          observer: true,
          slidesPerView: 5,
          slidesPerGroup: 5,
          spaceBetween: 15,
          navigation: {
            nextEl: widget.querySelector('.swiper-button-next'),
            prevEl: widget.querySelector('.swiper-button-prev'),
          },
          scrollbar: {
            el: widget.querySelector('.swiper-scrollbar'),
            draggable: true,
            dragSize: 182,
            snapOnRelease: false,
          },
          breakpoints: {
            1200: {
              slidesPerView: 4,
            },
            839: {
              slidesPerView: 4,
            },
            640: {
              slidesPerView: 3,
            },
            480: {
              slidesPerView: 2,
            },
          }
        },
      });
      carousel.render();
    }

    return {
      postRenderFn: postRenderFn,
      preRenderFn: preRenderFn,
    };
  }());

  retailrocket.widget.render('rr-widget-5c936b1697a5250c04c71061-personal');
}(retailrocket));

    (function check() {
      var result = exitCondition();
      if (result) {
        callback(result);
        return;
      }

      if (checkCount === 0) {
        if (force) {
          callback();
        }
        return;
      }

      checkCount -= 1;
      setTimeout(check, timeout);
    }());
  };

  var СarouselModel = function (sliderArguments) {

      wrapSelector: '.retailrocket-items',
      carouselSettings: {},

    function render(callback) {

        var extend = function(out) {
          out = out || {};

          for (var i = 1; i < arguments.length; i++) {
            if (!arguments[i])
              continue;

            for (var key in arguments[i]) {
              if (arguments[i].hasOwnProperty(key))
                out[key] = arguments[i][key];
            }
          }

          return out;
        };

        var settings = extend({}, defaultArguments, sliderArguments);

              $slideSiblings.forEach(function (sItem,index) {
                sItem.setAttribute('rrindex',(index + 1))
              });

              var index = slideItem.getAttribute('rrindex');

              if (index !== -1) {
                carousel.removeSlide(index);
              }
            });
          });
        });

        carousel.init();

        if (typeof callback === 'function') {
          callback(carousel);
        }
      }

      waitFor(
        function () {
          return window.RRSwiper;
        },
        function () {
          carouselInit();
        }
      );
    }

      throw new Error('The widget is required');
    }

    this.render = render;
  };

      }

      function add(id) {
        savedIds = savedIds.concat(id);
      }

      function remove(id) {
        var ids = [].concat(id);

        savedIds = savedIds.filter(function (savedId) {
          return ids.indexOf(savedId) === -1;
        });
      }

      function filter(recom) {
        if (!recom[paramName]) {
          return true;
        }

        if (!has(recom[paramName])) {
          add(recom[paramName]);
          return true;
        }

        return false;
      }

      function filteredCount(recoms) {
        return recoms.reduce(function (acc, recom) {
          return (acc + (has(recom[paramName]) ? 1 : 0));
        }, 0);
      }

      function getItems() {
        return savedIds.slice();
      }

      return {
        has: has,
        add: add,
        remove: remove,
        filter: filter,
        filteredCount: filteredCount,
        getItems: getItems,
      };
    });
  }

<script type="text/javascript">
(function (retailrocket) {
  'use strict';

  var waitFor = function (exitCondition, callback, force) {
    var checkCount = 100;
    var timeout = 1000;

    (function check() {
      var result = exitCondition();
      if (result) {
        callback(result);
        return;
      }

      if (checkCount === 0) {
        if (force) {
          callback();
        }
        return;
      }

      checkCount -= 1;
      setTimeout(check, timeout);
    }());
  };

  retailrocket['meleon5cc1672297a5281dc8160aea'] = (function () {
    waitFor(
      function () {
        return window.jQuery;
      },
      function ($) {
        $ = window.jQuery
        var button = $('#storetrigger-mailrequest-from-send-btn');
        var label = $('.retailrocket-subscribe-input-checkbox label');
        var checkbox = $('#subscribe-on-trigger-email');
        var emailForm = $('#storetrigger-mailrequest-form-email');
        var isValidEmail = false;

        emailForm.on('input',function() {
          if($(this).val() != '') {
            var pattern = /^([a-z0-9_\.-])+@[a-z0-9-]+\.([a-z]{2,4}\.)?[a-z]{2,4}$/i;
            if(pattern.test($(this).val()) && checkbox.is(':checked')){
              isValidEmail = true;
              button.removeAttr('disabled');
              button.removeClass('desabledButton');
            } else {
              button.addClass('desabledButton');
              button.attr('disabled', 'disabled');
              isValidEmail = false;
            }
          } else {
            button.addClass('desabledButton');
            button.attr('disabled', 'disabled');
            isValidEmail = false;
          }
        });

        checkbox.change(function() {
          if (checkbox.is(':checked')) {
            button.removeClass('desabledButton');
            button.removeAttr('disabled');
          } else {
            button.addClass('desabledButton');
            button.attr('disabled', 'disabled');
          }
        });
      }
    );

    function onUpdateFn(widget) {
      Number(widget.getAttribute('data-number-of-rendered-items')) >= 1 ? widget.parentElement.style.display = "block": widget.parentElement.style.display = "none";
    }

    return {
      onUpdateFn: onUpdateFn
    }
  }());

  retailrocket.exitIntendWidget.initialize();
  retailrocket.mailRequestForm.initialize();
  retailrocket.deactivateIfAgreedToReceiveMarketingMail.initialize();
  retailrocket.widget.render('rr-widget-5cc1672297a5281dc8160aea');
}(retailrocket));
</script>
